Running from 22–27 October, the programme celebrates local traditions while encouraging longer stays, visitor spending and evening exploration along the scenic Mekong riverfront. The post Amazing Mekong by Amazing Thailand elevates 2026 Nakhon Phanom Illuminated Boat Procession appeared first on TAT Newsroom .
